p. 1a) oṃ namaḥ śrīkubjikāyai || 
aiṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ hsphreṁ hsauṁ śrīgurupādukāṃ pūjayāmi ||
atha śrīkujāyā parāyāḥ pūjāvidhillikhyate || 
tritatvena trirācamya || tataḥ sūryyārgha || 
aiṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ hsphreṁ hsauṁ rhrkṣrauṁ kulamārttaṇḍabhairavāya 
prakāśaśaktisahitāya arghaṃ pā pūja || japastotra || 
aiṁ 5 kalikaluṣa kṛtāntaścaṇḍamārttaṇḍa bhīmo rajasakulakulāri 
jñānasaukhyapradātā || tṛtivatarama

p. 1b) nūnāṃ tvaṃ ca janetārghahastā kusumaphala 
jālārghattvā"nkulārkkannamāmi || kulamārttaṇḍabhairavāya puṣpaṃ namaḥ ||

p. 3b) aiṁ 5 kiṇi 2 vicce koṅkannāya hraḥ astrāya phaṭ karatalapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ pā 
pū 5 || iti karanyāsaḥ ||

aiṁ 5 hsphreṁ kubjikāyai pūrvvavaktrāya pā pū 5 ||

iti vaktranyāsaḥ ||

aiṁ 5 namo bhagavati hṛtakamalāyai

p 4a) hrāṁ śrī hṛdayāya namaḥ pā pū 5 ||
aiṁ 5 hsphreṁ kubjikāyai kuladvīpāyai hrīṁ śrīṁ śirase svāhā pā pū ||
aiṁ 5 hrāṁ hrīṁ hrūṁ varvvaraśikhe hrū śrīśikhāyai vauṣaṭ pā pū 5 ||
aiṁ 5 ghore aghore aghorāmukhī bahurūpāyai hrai śrī kavacāya hūṁ pā pū 5 ||
aiṁ 5 chāṁ chīṁ mahantārikāyai hrauṁ śrī netratrayāya vaṣaṭ pā pū 5 ||
aiṁ 5 kiṇi 2 vicce koṅkannāya hraḥ astrāya phaṭ pā pū 5 ||
ityaṅganyāsaḥ ||

tato pātrasthāpanaṃ kuryyāt || ta bhūmau caturasramaṇḍalaṃ vilikhye || 
hrīṁ ādhāraśaktyādibhyo

p. 4b) namaḥ ityāsana || astramantreṇa prakṣālya || aghoreṇāgnau guggulaṃ hutvā 
|| hrīṁ vajrodake hūṁ phaṭ || vajreṇa dhūpayet || candanādi saṃpūjya || 
tatra bhūmau saṃsthāpya nama iti mantreṇa vāriṇā pūrayet || tataḥ pūjanaṃ ||
aiṁ pā pū 5 || hrīṁ pā pū 5 || śrīṁ pā pū 5 || hsphreṁ pā pū 5 || hsauṁ pā pū 5 || 
aiṁ 5 hskṣmlvryūṁ ānandaśakti bhairavānanda vīrādhipataye śkṣmlvryīṁ 
jalapātra pā pū 5 || hrāṁ hṛdayādibhiḥ saṃpūjya || 
āvāhanādi candanākṣata puṣpaṃ namaḥ || dhenumudrāṃ darśayet ||

p. 5a) gāyatrī || 
aiṁ 5 kubjikāyai vidmahe kuladvīpāyai dhīmahī tanno kubjiḥ pracodayāt ||
devānmānaṃ tajjalena siñcanaṃ || 
iti jalapātrasthāpanaṃ vidhiḥ ||

punaḥ arghāpātrasthāpanaṃ || thavanajavasa trikoṇacākapekacoya || thepūjā ||
hrīṁ ādhāraśaktyādibhyo namaḥ || thvate pūjāyāṅāva arghānāṃ macino 
astreṇa prakṣālya || maṇḍalasate || laṃkhathanne namadhakaṃ ||
oṃ mantranaceta svānate || 

oṃ gaṃge ca jamune caiva goḍāvalī sarasvatī |
 narmmade sindhu kāveri jale'smin saṃnidhā bhava ||

p. 5b) aṃkuśamudrāna sūryyamaṇḍalasacoṅatīrtha āvāhanayāya ||
oṃ dhāra 10 japaraye || vajreṇa dhūpakene || iti arghāsthāpanaṃ ||

tato śaṃkhasthāpanaṃ || 
thavakhavasavasalaṃkhanahāyāva trikoṇa cākapekacoyathe pūjāyāya || 
oṃ hrīṁ śaṃkhadhāra maṇḍalāya namaḥ || astramantreṇa maciserāvamaṇḍala 
sasthāpanayāya || thepūjā || mantra || 

oṃ maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya daśakalātmane śaṃkhādhārāya namaḥ || 
astreṇa śaṃkhaselāvamaṃcisate || aiṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ hsphreṁ hsauṁ śaṃkhasthāpayāmi 
|| pūjā ||

p. 6a) oṃ aṃ arkkamaṇḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane śaṃkhārghapātrāya namaḥ ||
laṃkhathanne || blūṁ jalapūrayāmi namaḥ || aṃkuśamudrāṇatīrtha 
āvāhanayāya || oṃ gaṃgeti mantreṇa || pūjā || oṃ 
somamaṇḍalāya ṣoḍaśakalātmane śaṃkhārghāmṛtāya namaḥ ||
hsauṁ varuṇā devi śauṁ varuṇādevyai namaḥ || namadhakaceta svāna akṣata 
dūrvvāte || vajreṇa dhūpayet || hrāṁ hṛdayādibhiḥ saṃpūjya || vauṣaṭ || 
matsyamudrāṇayāya || vaṃ dhenumudrā || tāratraya digbandhanaṃyāya || 
tho laṃkhanadhamano pūjā sāmāgrīnohāya || kubji

p. 6b) gāyatrī || iti śaṃkhasthāpanaṃ ||

atha ghaṭasthāpanaṃ ||
svavāme bindu trikoṇaṃ vilikhya || tatra pūjāyathā || 
aiṁ 5 raṁ agnimaṇḍalāya namaḥ || 
aiṁ 5 kiṇi 2 vicce koṅkannāya hraḥ astrāya phaṭ ||
tatmantreṇa ghaṭaṃ prakṣālya || raktacandanena vilipya || 
tanmaṇḍale saṃsthāpya pūjayet || 
aiṁ 5 haṃ sūryyamaṇḍalāya namaḥ || 
iti saṃpūjya vatīśi mantramuccaran ghaṭaṃ pūjayet || pūjanaṃ ||
aiṁ 5 saṁ somamaṇḍalāya namaḥ || sveṣṭadevatāyā mūlamantreṇa pūjanaṃ ||

tato matsyamudrāyācchādya || 
aiṁ plūṁ hsauṁ haṁ 

p. 7a) saṁḥ amṛte 2 amṛtodbhave amṛtavarṣiṇī amṛtaṃ śrāvaya svāhā ||
anenamantreṇa daśadhā japtvā ||
aiṁ 5 glūṁ mlūṁ plūṁ slūṁ nlūṁ paṃcaratnāyai namaḥ ||
aiṁ 5 aiṁ plūṁ hsauṁ haṁ saḥ amṛte 2 amṛtodabhave amṛtavarṣṣiṇī amṛtaṃ śrāvaya 
2 sauṁ sudhā śukra śrāpaṃ mocaya 2 caturaṃ niyināṃ siddhi sāmarthya daha 2 
mahākhecarī mudrāṃ prakaṭaya 2 hūṁ svāhā || 
aiṁ 5 klāṁ klīṁ klūṁ amṛte 2 amṛtodbhave amṛtavarṣiṇī hrīṁ amṛtaṃ śrāvaya 2 
hrīṁ sudhā? kṛṣṇaśrāpa vimocaya 2 kṛṣṇaśrāpa? mocakāyai hūṁ 2 svāhā ||
aiṁ 5 haṁ 9 ānandabhairavāya saṁ 9 ānandabhairavī vauṣaṭ ||
mūlamantreṇa saptadhā japtvā dhenuyonimudrāṃ darśayediti ghaṭasthāpanaṃ || 

(śuddhi mīnamudrā kulāmṛtaśodhanaṃ || thana dravyaśodhanasargā *? ||)

tatra pūjayedyathā ||
aiṁ 5 kāmarūpapīṭhāya namaḥ || aiṁ 5 pūrṇagiripīṭhāya namaḥ ||
aiṁ 5 jālandharapīṭhāya namaḥ || aiṁ 5 oḍiyānapīṭhāya namaḥ ||

p. 12a) trinetraṃ piṅgorddhakeśamasi pheṭakarantathā |
vaiṇa kaṅkālahastañca triśūla khaṭvāṅgameva ca ||

bindupātradharandevaṃ kṣetrapāla baṭukantathā ||
dhyāna puṣpannamaḥ ||

aiṁ 5 devīputra baṭukanātha mahākapila trijaṭā bhārabhāsura trinetra jvālāmukha

p. 12b) khaḍga pheṭakahastañca kapāla khaṭvāṅgameva ca ||

aiṁ 5 ihaiva sthāpanādhipataye yathā nāmne atra maṇḍalamadhye avatara 2

p. 13a) mama vighnocchādaya 2 hrāṁ hrīṁ hrūṁ phaṭ svāhā || evaṃ bali || kṣetra ||
aiṁ 5 siṃhāsanāya pā pū 5 || 

dhyānaṃ ||
aiṁ 5 taptakāñcanavarṇābhā kāmarūpī suyauvanā |
mattārūpī ca kaṅkāla sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitā ||

liṅgaṃ pāśaṃ kāpālañca cāṃkuśaṃ karaśobhanī |
pretañcaiva samāsthāya sarvvakāmārthasiddhidā ||
dhyānapuṣpaṃ pā pū 5 ||

aiṁ 5 ye kecidyoginīnāṃ pīṭhajā kṣetrajā sahajā yogajā mantrajā garbhajā 
kulajā ye kecidyoginīnāṃ khecarī bhūcarī gocarī ekākṣarī dvekṣarī tryakṣarī

p. 13b) pañca ṣaṭ saptāṣṭa navākṣarīṇāṃ trijagadvāsinīnāṃ 
yathopapannānnaṃ baliṃ gṛhna 2 mama siddhiṅkurvvantu hūṁ 3 phaṭ svāhā || 
evaṃ bali || yoginī ||

aiṁ 5 pretāsana pā pū 5 || 

dhyānaṃ ||
aiṁ 5 ghorarūpātiraudrī ca caṇḍarūpāticaṇḍikā |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanā pretasyoparisaṃsthitā ||

nirmmāsaṃ kālarātrī ca muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitā |
khaḍga pheṭakadharī devī ḍamarū khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇī ||

p. 130a) tato ṣaḍāmnāya devayā dhyānacosya tayādu ||

aiṁ hrīṁ khhphrīṁ indranīla samaprakhya daśadorghaṇḍamanditaṃ |
varābhayakaraṃ ramya satriśūlaṃ kapālakaṃ ||

ḍamaruṃ cākṣamālā ca śaktiṃ caiva karāmbuje |
ikṣucāpaṃ śaraṃ caiva khaḍgahastaṃ suśobhitaṃ ||

sarvvābharaṇabhūṣāṅgī pūrṇeśī paricintayet |
mantra ū || pūrvvayā || dakṣiṇāmnāya ||

aiṁ hrīṁ khhsphreṁ sahasrādityasaṃkāśāṃ yauvano jvala ṣaṭbhujāṃ |
pāśāṃkuśa kapālañca mudrā vārābhayodyatāṃ |

muditā siddhya medhuno śaktibhiḥ parivātāṃ |
sarvvālaṃkāraśobhitāṃ niśyeśī parameśvarī || 
mantra ū || 

paścimāmnāya ||
aiṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ hsphreṁ hsauṁ ākāśābhaṃ trinetraṃ vṛṣabhatanugataṃ paṭṭavastraṃ 
smitāsyaṃ
caikaṃ dorbhirddadhānaṃ ḍamaruka paraśuṃ maṇḍalāgreṣu vajrān | 
śaṃkhaṃ khaṭvāṅga pāśāṅkuśa phalakavarābhīti khaḍga triśūlan 
cāpaṃ  ghaṇṭākapālaṃ sajaṭa śaśidharāṃ vallakīṃ vādayantī || 

taruṇaravinibhāsyāṃ siṃhapṛṣṭoya viṣṭāṃ kucabharanamitāṃgī 
sarvvabhūṣābhirāmāṃ |

p. 130b) abhaya varadahastāmekavaktrā trinetrā madamudita mukhābjāṃ 
kubjikāṃ cintayāmi || 

mantra ūṃ || 

uttārāmnāya ||
aiṁ hrīṁ phreṁ nīlavarṇa mahākāli trimukhānavalocanā |
pītaṃ tu dakṣiṇe vaktraṃ muttara pāvakopamī ||

maṇḍalaṃmbitanetrā ca aṣṭabāhu tanudharā |
piṅgalorddha jaṭātopaṃ candrārdhakṛtaśekharā ||

saptāviṃśatī mutraiśca raktaviṣṇupasaṃyutā |
asthikuṇḍalaśobhāḍhyā maulau kapālamālinī ||

kiṅkinī nūpurārambi viraghaṇṭhāvarottamā |
śivapreta samārūḍhā mukhaśonita nirjharā ||

indrādyā brahmaviṣṇuśca rudreśvara sadāśivaḥ |
pītakṛṣṇaśyāmaraktadhūmrābhā śvetakārakāḥ ||

vajradaṇḍaśakti cakraśūlakhaṭvāṅgadhārakāḥ |
tarjanī mukhanikṣiptāḥ śivadhaḥsthā trilocanā ||

vaddha padmāsanā devi pīnonnata payodharā |
vāmahaste kapālaṃ ca raktapūrṇāti śobhanaṃ ||

dhārayantī triśūlaṃ ca saśabdaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
vajra tu dakṣiṇe haste aṃkuśaṃ mudgaraṃ tathā ||

vāme pāśaṃ ca khaṭvāṅga mṛtacālaṃ ca dhāriṇī |

p. 131) citkalākalamadhyasthā jyotirūpā ca kevalā ||

nirvvāṇapadadātitā bhāvātimagocarāṃ |
asminmūrtto mahādevyā navavaktrāmi dhyāpayet ||

bhogādhikāra bhedena traya triśākṣarī yajet |
asyā devyāḥ parārūpā navavaktrāṃ prakīrtitā ||
mantraṣoḍaśākṣara uṃ ||

siddhilakṣmī ||
aiṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ pūrṇendu dyuti saṃkhakundadhavarāṃ khandendu yuktā jaṭā 
netraiḥ paṃcadaśaivvirājita parāṃ brahmātmikāṃ sundarī |
rudrasyoparivaktrapañcaka yutāṃ śūlāsi muṇḍāṃkuśaṃ 
khaṭvāṅgaṃ ca sapātra pāśa kalaśaṃ hastauḥ prasādābhayaṃ ||
mūlamālāmantra uṃ ||

ūddhvāmnāya ||
aiṁ klīṁ sauṁḥ sūryandvagnimayaika pīṭhanilayaṃ bālārkka vicāruṇāṃ 
trikṣācandrakalāvataṃ samukuṭāṃ pīnastanīṃ sundarīṃ |
pāśacāṅkuśamikṣu cāpavidhṛtāṃ puṣpeṣu hastāparāṃ 
nānābhūṣaṇabhūṣitāṃti kusumārāṅgīṃ bhajedvaindave || 

turīye bījeti mantra uṃ || adhāmnāya || aiṁ hrīṁ khphre

p. 132) kalaśayā ||
aiṁ 5 candramaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ śuddhasphaṭikanirmmalaṃ |
gokṣīrasadṛśaṃ devaṃ kuṇḍaccandana sundarī ||

catubhujaṃ mahātejaṃ netratritayasobhitaṃ |
jaṭājūṭadharaṃ nityaṃ khaṇḍendu kṛtaśeṣaraṃ ||

sarvvaccayavasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvvābharaṇabhūṣīṇaṃ |
vāmahaste śudhākumbhaṃ dakṣiṇe akṣamālikā ||

pustakammṛṇālahastañca dhārayantaṃ sadoditaṃ |
sarvvadevavaravaṃde divyavastraparicchedaṃ ||

sarvvamṛtyuprasamanaṃ sarvvasiddhiphalapradaṃ |
mahālakṣmī yate dhyāyat tadaṃ mṛtyeśvarabhairavaṃ ||
mantra uṃ ||
aiṁ 5 padmasiṃhāsanādevī javādāḍimarāgiṇī |
lākṣārasanibhā devī padmarāga samaprabhāṃ ||

ekavaktra trinetrāñca divyālaṃkālabhūṣitā |
khaḍgapheṭaka hastā ca pūrṇapātrasabindudhā ||

padmāsanasukhāsīnāṃ saumyarūpāvatāraṇī |
evaṃ dhyātvā mahādevī vāruṇī kubjikeśvarī || 
mantra uṃ || kuṃbhayā ||

kaumārīyā || 
oṃ baṃdhukāruṇasaṃkāśā taḍitkoṭi samaprabhā |
caturbhujāṃ varhinasthā kaumārī kulamātaraṃ || mantra ūṃ || iti dhyāṃ ||

p. 133a) oṃ namo gurave || atha mantranimittaṃ matimānmāsādīni pariśodhayet ||

caitra duḥkhāya dīkṣāsyādvaiśāṣe ratnasaṃcayaḥ |
maraṇaṃ ca bhavati jaiṣṭhe tvākhāḍhe bandhunāśanaṃ ||

samṛddhiṃ śrāvaṇe nūnaṃ bhavedbhādrapade kṣayaṃ |
prajānāmāśvine māse dīkṣā sarvvaphalapradā ||

jñānaṃ syāt kārttike saukhyaṃ mārgaśīrṣe dhanaṃ haret |
pauṣe jñānakṣayaṃ māghe bhavenmedhāvivardhanaṃ ||

phālguṇe sarvvasiddhisyātmalamāsaṃ vivarjjayet |
yattra mantrārambhastu caitra svātmamastapuruṣārthamiti ||

gautamīyavacanaṃ || tadvaiṣṇavaparamiti ||

tatra yāmale ||
asiddhirbhūmisaṃpattirmmaraṇaṃ bandhunāśanaṃ |
āyurvvṛddhi prajānāśaḥ saṃpatti ratna saṃcayaḥ ||

pūrṇaphala śatruvṛddhirmmedhā kāmaprapūrṇatā |
caitrādīnāṃ phalaṃ jñeyaṃ malamāsaṃ vivarjjayet ||

vāraniyamaḥ ||
ravivāre bhavedduḥkha saume śānti pradā bhavet |
āyuraṅgārako hanti dīkṣāṃ tatra vivarjjayet ||

budhe saundaryyamāpnoti jñānaṃ syāttu bṛhaspatau |
śukla saubhā māpnoti paśūhāni śaniścare ||

atha tithiniyamaḥ ||
caturthī navamī caiva caturddaśyāṣṭamī tathā |
pūrṇimā pañcamī caiva dvitīyā saptamī tathā ||

trayodaśī ca daśamī praśastā sarvvasiddhidā |
śuklapakṣe śubhā dīkṣā kṛṣṇepi pañcamāhnikān ||

mukti kāmaiḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe bhūmi kāmaiḥ śite tathā |
kṛṣṇāṣṭambāṃ caturddaśyāṃ dīkṣākarmma śubhapradaṃ ||

yattu vaiṣṇavatantra ||
patipaddvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ śucirbhavet |
catuthyāṃ vittanāśaḥ syāt pañcamyāṃ buddhivarddhanaṃ ||

ṣaṣṭhāṃ jñānakṣayaḥ saukhyaṃ saṃbhavet saptamī dine |

p. 133b) aṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśaḥ syāt navamyāṃ vapuṣaḥ kṣayaḥ ||

daśamyāṃ rājasaubhāgyamekādaśyāṃ śucirbhavet |
dvādaśyāṃ sarvvasiddhiḥ syāttrayodaśyāṃ daridratā ||

tīryyagayoniścaturdaśyāṃ hāniścāpyavasānake |
pakṣānte dharmmavṛddhiḥ syāt svādhyāyaṃ ca vivarjjayet ||

saṃdhyā garjjita nirghoṣaṃ bhūkampoktā nipātane |
tatrāna dhyāyāṃśca divasān śrutyuktān parivarjjayet ||

iti vaiṣṇava paramati ||

atha nakṣatraniyamaḥ ||

aśvinyāṃ sukhamāpnoti bharaṇyāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvaṃ |
kṛttikāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī rohiṇyāṃ vākyati bhavet ||

mṛgaśīrṣa sukhāvāptirādrāyāṃ bandunāśanaṃ |
punarvvasau bhavedduḥkhī puṣpāyāṃ dhanavān bhavet ||

aśleṣāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī maghāyāṃ kṣayamāpnuyāt |
phālguṇe dvitaye caiva paśorvvṛddhirbhavet kila ||

hastāyāṃ dhanahānisyāccitra svātyoḥ sukhī bhavet | 
viśakhāyāṃ sukhaṃ cānurādhāyāṃ bandhunāśanaṃ ||

jyeṣṭhāyāṃ sutavṛddhiḥ syānmūlāyāṃ kīrttivarddhanaṃ |
pūrvvakhāḍhottarāṣāḍhe bhavetāṃ kīrti dāyike ||

śravaṇāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī dhaniṣṭhāyāṃ daridratā |
buddhiḥ śatabhiṣāyāṃ syāt pūrvabhādre daridratā ||

saukhyaṃ cottarābhādre ca revatyāṃ kīrtivarddhanaṃ ||

lagnaniyamaḥ ||
vṛsasiṃhe ca kanyāyāṃ dhanurmmīnākhya lagnake |
candratārānukule ca kuryyāddīkṣā pravarttanaṃ ||

tṛtīyaikādaśa ṣaṣṭhadaśamasthaścandramā |
janmacandraḥ śubhaḥ prokto janmanakṣatra varjjitaḥ ||

śuklapakṣe dvitīyastu pañcamo navamastathā |
gocare ye gṛhāḥ śuddhā tairyuktāpi śubhā śaśī ||

śubha madhya sthito vāpi candramā śubhadā bhavet |

p. 134a) tṛtīyā saptamī caiva ṣoḍaśāṣṭadaśe tathā ||

unaviṃśaika ca viṃśe ca trayoviṃśe tathaiva ca |
paṃcaviṃsati nakṣatre śubhakarmmāṇi varjjayet ||

rudrayāmale ||
paṃcāṅgaśuddhidivase śobhane śaśitārayoḥ |
guruśukrodaye caiva śasyate mantra satkriyā ||

pañcāgeti || 
yogakaraṇaṃ tithivārāṇāṃ suddhā nityarthaḥ ||

vārāhītantre || 
śukrāṣṭau yadi vā vṛddhau gurvvābhye bhaved yadi |
meṣavṛścikasiṃheṣu tadā doṣo na vidyate ||

atra yadyapi śuddhabhādrapadasyāpi niṣiddhastathāpi bhādrapado pādānaṃ 
sāmānyatvapratipādanārthena kevalaṃ dīkṣāyāṃ pratiprakāraḥ | kiṃtu 
sarvvakarmmāścini ||

ratnāvalyāṃ ||
yogaśca prītirāyuṣmān saubhāgyaśobhanastathā |
vṛddhidhruvaḥ sukarmmā ca sādhyaḥ śuklaśca harṣaṇaḥ ||

variyāścānaraḥ siddho brahma aindraśca ṣoḍaśaḥ |
śubhāni karaṇāni syurddikṣāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||

śakunyādīnidhiṣṭiñca viśeṣa vivarjjayet ||

yoginīhṛdaye ||
yasmātsa tithivārādi śodhanaṃ proktā me vahi |
mantradīkṣā paraṃ tattu vijñayaṃ sādhaka sattamaiḥ ||

vidyādīkṣāparaṃ caiva tithimāsādiśodhanaṃ |
yadi vecchā tadā dīkṣā gurorājñānusārataḥ ||

ṭiṣuvepyayane caiva ākhāḍhyāṃ madanotsave |
dīkṣākāryyā prayatnena pavitraṃ guruparvvaṇi ||

guruparvvaniyamastatraiva ||

ṣaṣṭhī bhādrapade māsi iṣe kṛṣṇe caturddaśī |
kārtike navamī śuklā mārgge śukla dvitīyakā ||

p. 134b) pauṣe ca navamī śuklā māghe vara caturthikā |
phālguṇe navamī śuklā caitre kāmatrayodaśī ||

vaiśāṣe cākṣayā śastā jyaiṣṭhe daśaharā tathā |
śucau ca pañcamīśuklā śrāvaṇe kṛṣṇapaṃcamī ||

etāni guruparvvāṇi tīrtha koṭiphalaṃ labhet |
ninditeṣvapi māseṣu dīkṣoktā grahane śubhe ||

sūryyagrahaṇa kālasya samāno nāsti bhūtale |
tatra yadyat kṛtaṃ karmma tadanantaphalaṃ labhet ||

śaktiyāmale ||
candragraha sahasreṇa sūryyagraha samo bhavet |
tato lakṣaguṇaṃ proktaṃ grahaṇe nijavāsare ||

yoginīhṛdaye ca ||
ravisaṃkramaṇe caiva sūryyasya grahaṇe tathā |
tatra lagnādikaṃ kicitnavicārayet kadācana ||

mahāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣeṇa kālākālaṃ viśodhayet ||

iti dīkṣātithiphalaṃ ||

atropyatoktaḥ kaściccedabjamabhyarccāsākṣataṃ |
tadambu nābhiṣiṃcyāṣṭa vālena kevalaṃ ||

nidhāyāṣṭau japet karṇe upadeśe tvayaṃ vidhiḥ |
candrasūryyagrahe tīrthe siddhikṣetre śivālaye ||

mantramātra prakathanamupadeśaḥ sa ucyate || 
ityupadeśaḥ ||

mahāniśi caturddaśyāṃmuparāge cāśvinīṃ | 
dadanmantraṃ bhavenmantrī daridraḥ saptajanmasu ||

caturddaśī mahāpuṇyā kuhuyuktā bhayāvahā |
nasākāryyā gṛhesthena putrapautra dhanāpahā ||

śivāghorātarā pretā sāvitrī ca caturddaśī |
kuhū yuktaiva karttavyā kuhvāmapi ca pālaṇaṃ ||

śubhaḥ ||
